Soft Aurora









import SoftAurora from './SoftAurora';

<div style={{ width: '100%', height: '600px', position: 'relative' }}>
  <SoftAurora
    speed={0.6}
    scale={1.5}
    brightness={1}
    color1="#f7f7f7"
    color2="#e100ff"
    bandHeight={0.5}
    bandSpread={1}
    enableMouseInteraction
    mouseInfluence={0.25}
    spectrum={1}
    colorSpeed={1}
    noiseFrequency={2.5}
    noiseAmplitude={1}
    tilt={0}
    octaveDecay={0.1}
    layerOffset={0}
    clickPulse
    intro
    lightMode={false}
/>
</div>









'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import './SoftAurora.css';

const MAX_PULSES = 4;

const VERTEX = `#version 300 es
const vec2 corners[3] = vec2[3](vec2(-1.0, -1.0), vec2(3.0, -1.0), vec2(-1.0, 3.0));
void main() {
  gl_Position = vec4(corners[gl_VertexID], 0.0, 1.0);
}`;

const FRAGMENT = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
uniform vec2 uResolution;
uniform float uFlow;
uniform float uHue;
uniform float uScale;
uniform float uBrightness;
uniform vec3 uColor1;
uniform vec3 uColor2;
uniform float uNoiseFreq;
uniform float uNoiseAmp;
uniform float uBandHeight;
uniform float uBandSpread;
uniform float uOctaveDecay;
uniform float uLayerOffset;
uniform vec2 uShift;
uniform vec4 uCursor;
uniform vec4 uPulses[${MAX_PULSES}];
uniform float uSpectrum;
uniform float uTilt;
uniform vec3 uIntro;
uniform float uLightMode;
uniform float uOpacity;
out vec4 fragColor;

#define TAU 6.28318

vec3 gradientHash(vec3 p) {
  p = vec3(
    dot(p, vec3(127.1, 311.7, 234.6)),
    dot(p, vec3(269.5, 183.3, 198.3)),
    dot(p, vec3(169.5, 283.3, 156.9))
  );
  vec3 h = fract(sin(p) * 43758.5453123);
  float phi = acos(2.0 * h.x - 1.0);
  float theta = TAU * h.y;
  return vec3(cos(theta) * sin(phi), sin(theta) * cos(phi), cos(phi));
}

float quinticSmooth(float t) {
  float t2 = t * t;
  float t3 = t * t2;
  return 6.0 * t3 * t2 - 15.0 * t2 * t2 + 10.0 * t3;
}

vec3 cosineGradient(float t, vec3 a, vec3 b, vec3 c, vec3 d) {
  return a + b * cos(TAU * (c * t + d));
}

float perlin3D(float amplitude, float frequency, float px, float py, float pz) {
  float x = px * frequency;
  float y = py * frequency;

  float fx = floor(x);
  float fy = floor(y);
  float fz = floor(pz);
  float cx = ceil(x);
  float cy = ceil(y);
  float cz = ceil(pz);

  vec3 g000 = gradientHash(vec3(fx, fy, fz));
  vec3 g100 = gradientHash(vec3(cx, fy, fz));
  vec3 g010 = gradientHash(vec3(fx, cy, fz));
  vec3 g110 = gradientHash(vec3(cx, cy, fz));
  vec3 g001 = gradientHash(vec3(fx, fy, cz));
  vec3 g101 = gradientHash(vec3(cx, fy, cz));
  vec3 g011 = gradientHash(vec3(fx, cy, cz));
  vec3 g111 = gradientHash(vec3(cx, cy, cz));

  float d000 = dot(g000, vec3(x - fx, y - fy, pz - fz));
  float d100 = dot(g100, vec3(x - cx, y - fy, pz - fz));
  float d010 = dot(g010, vec3(x - fx, y - cy, pz - fz));
  float d110 = dot(g110, vec3(x - cx, y - cy, pz - fz));
  float d001 = dot(g001, vec3(x - fx, y - fy, pz - cz));
  float d101 = dot(g101, vec3(x - cx, y - fy, pz - cz));
  float d011 = dot(g011, vec3(x - fx, y - cy, pz - cz));
  float d111 = dot(g111, vec3(x - cx, y - cy, pz - cz));

  float sx = quinticSmooth(x - fx);
  float sy = quinticSmooth(y - fy);
  float sz = quinticSmooth(pz - fz);

  float lx00 = mix(d000, d100, sx);
  float lx10 = mix(d010, d110, sx);
  float lx01 = mix(d001, d101, sx);
  float lx11 = mix(d011, d111, sx);

  float ly0 = mix(lx00, lx10, sy);
  float ly1 = mix(lx01, lx11, sy);

  return amplitude * mix(ly0, ly1, sz);
}

float auroraGlow(float t, vec2 uv, float bend) {
  float noiseVal = 0.0;
  float freq = uNoiseFreq;
  float amp = uNoiseAmp;
  vec2 samplePos = uv * uScale;

  for (float i = 0.0; i < 3.0; i += 1.0) {
    noiseVal += perlin3D(amp, freq, samplePos.x, samplePos.y, t);
    amp *= uOctaveDecay;
    freq *= 2.0;
  }

  float yBand = uv.y * 10.0 - (uBandHeight + bend) * 10.0;
  return 0.3 * max(exp(uBandSpread * (1.0 - 1.1 * uIntro.y * abs(noiseVal * uIntro.z + yBand))), 0.0);
}

vec2 turn(vec2 p, vec2 center) {
  float c = cos(uTilt);
  float s = sin(uTilt);
  vec2 d = p - center;
  return center + vec2(c * d.x - s * d.y, s * d.x + c * d.y);
}

void main() {
  vec2 uv = gl_FragCoord.xy / uResolution.xy;
  vec2 center = vec2(0.5 * uResolution.x / uResolution.y, 0.5);
  vec2 screen = turn(gl_FragCoord.xy / uResolution.y, center);
  vec2 cursor = turn(uCursor.xy, center);
  vec2 field = screen + uShift;
  float t = 0.4 * uFlow;

  float reach = exp(-pow((screen.x - cursor.x) / 0.32, 2.0)) * uCursor.z;
  float gap = cursor.y + uShift.y - uBandHeight;
  float near = exp(-gap * gap / 0.16);
  float bend = reach * gap * near * 0.55;

  float pulse = 0.0;
  for (int i = 0; i < ${MAX_PULSES}; i++) {
    vec4 wave = uPulses[i];
    if (wave.z <= 0.0) continue;
    float x = (abs(screen.x - turn(wave.xy, center).x) - wave.w * 0.55) / (0.1 + wave.w * 0.06);
    pulse += wave.z * exp(-x * x);
  }
  float boost = (1.0 + reach * near * 0.25 + pulse * 0.65) * uIntro.x;

  float glow1 = auroraGlow(t, field, bend) * boost;
  float glow2 = auroraGlow(t + uLayerOffset, field, bend) * boost;
  vec3 gradient1 = mix(vec3(1.0), cosineGradient(uv.x + uHue * 0.2, vec3(0.5), vec3(0.5), vec3(1.0), vec3(0.3, 0.20, 0.20)), uSpectrum);
  vec3 gradient2 = mix(vec3(1.0), cosineGradient(uv.x + uHue * 0.1, vec3(0.5), vec3(0.5), vec3(2.0, 1.0, 0.0), vec3(0.5, 0.20, 0.25)), uSpectrum);

  vec3 col = 0.99 * glow1 * gradient1 * uColor1;
  col += 0.99 * glow2 * gradient2 * uColor2;
  col *= uBrightness;

  float grain = (fract(52.9829189 * fract(dot(gl_FragCoord.xy, vec2(0.06711056, 0.00583715)))) - 0.5) / 255.0;
  if (uLightMode > 0.5) {
    float energy = max(col.r, max(col.g, col.b));
    vec3 hue = clamp(col / max(energy, 1e-4), 0.0, 1.0);
    vec3 average = (glow1 * uColor1 + glow2 * uColor2) / max(glow1 + glow2, 1e-4);
    float top = max(average.r, max(average.g, average.b));
    float spread = top - min(average.r, min(average.g, average.b));
    float core = smoothstep(0.55, 1.6, energy);
    float depth = 1.25 + 0.45 * core;
    vec3 tint = mix(average * 0.82, pow(average / max(top, 1e-4), vec3(depth + 0.6)), smoothstep(0.04, 0.2, spread));
    float vivid = smoothstep(0.12, 0.55, 1.0 - min(hue.r, min(hue.g, hue.b))) * smoothstep(0.04, 0.2, spread);
    vec3 ink = mix(tint, pow(hue, vec3(depth)), vivid) * mix(0.93, 0.8, core);
    float density = 0.88 * (1.0 - exp(-1.9 * pow(energy, 1.7)));
    density = clamp(density * uOpacity + grain, 0.0, 1.0);
    fragColor = vec4(ink * density, density);
  } else {
    float alpha = clamp(length(col), 0.0, 1.0);
    vec3 color = clamp(col, 0.0, 1.0);
    fragColor = vec4(clamp(color * alpha + grain, 0.0, alpha) * uOpacity, alpha * uOpacity);
  }
}`;

const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

const parseColor = value => {
  const text = String(value || '').trim();
  const hex = /^#?([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(text);
  if (hex) {
    const full = hex[1].length === 3 ? hex[1].replace(/./g, digit => digit + digit) : hex[1];
    return [0, 2, 4].map(index => parseInt(full.slice(index, index + 2), 16) / 255);
  }
  const rgb = /^rgba?\(\s*([\d.]+)[\s,]+([\d.]+)[\s,]+([\d.]+)/i.exec(text);
  if (rgb) return [rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3]].map(channel => clamp(parseFloat(channel) / 255, 0, 1));
  return null;
};

const compile = (gl, type, source) => {
  const shader = gl.createShader(type);
  if (!shader) return null;
  gl.shaderSource(shader, source);
  gl.compileShader(shader);
  if (!gl.getShaderParameter(shader, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) {
    console.error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(shader));
    gl.deleteShader(shader);
    return null;
  }
  return shader;
};

const link = gl => {
  const vertex = compile(gl, gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX);
  const fragment = compile(gl, gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAGMENT);
  const program = vertex && fragment ? gl.createProgram() : null;
  if (!vertex || !fragment || !program) {
    if (vertex) gl.deleteShader(vertex);
    if (fragment) gl.deleteShader(fragment);
    return null;
  }
  gl.attachShader(program, vertex);
  gl.attachShader(program, fragment);
  gl.linkProgram(program);
  gl.deleteShader(vertex);
  gl.deleteShader(fragment);
  if (!gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.LINK_STATUS)) {
    console.error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(program));
    gl.deleteProgram(program);
    return null;
  }
  const uniforms = {};
  const count = gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.ACTIVE_UNIFORMS);
  for (let index = 0; index < count; index++) {
    const info = gl.getActiveUniform(program, index);
    if (info) uniforms[info.name.replace(/\[0\]$/, '')] = gl.getUniformLocation(program, info.name);
  }
  return { program, uniforms };
};

const smoother = t => {
  const x = clamp(t, 0, 1);
  return x * x * x * (x * (x * 6 - 15) + 10);
};

const SoftAurora = ({
  speed = 0.6,
  scale = 1.5,
  brightness = 1.0,
  color1 = '#f7f7f7',
  color2 = '#e100ff',
  noiseFrequency = 2.5,
  noiseAmplitude = 1.0,
  bandHeight = 0.5,
  bandSpread = 1.0,
  octaveDecay = 0.1,
  layerOffset = 0,
  colorSpeed = 1.0,
  spectrum = 1,
  tilt = 0,
  enableMouseInteraction = true,
  mouseInfluence = 0.25,
  clickPulse = true,
  intro = true,
  lightMode = false,
  opacity = 1,
  className = '',
  style
}) => {
  const containerRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const wakeRef = useRef(null);
  const [generation, setGeneration] = useState(0);

  const settings = {
    speed: Math.max(0, speed),
    scale: Math.max(0.01, scale),
    brightness: Math.max(0, brightness),
    color1: parseColor(color1) || [0.969, 0.969, 0.969],
    color2: parseColor(color2) || [0.882, 0, 1],
    noiseFrequency: Math.max(0.01, noiseFrequency),
    noiseAmplitude: Math.max(0, noiseAmplitude),
    bandHeight,
    bandSpread: Math.max(0, bandSpread),
    octaveDecay: Math.max(0, octaveDecay),
    layerOffset,
    colorSpeed,
    spectrum: clamp(spectrum, 0, 1),
    tilt: (clamp(tilt, -90, 90) * Math.PI) / 180,
    mouse: enableMouseInteraction,
    influence: Math.max(0, mouseInfluence),
    clickPulse,
    intro,
    light: lightMode,
    opacity: clamp(opacity, 0, 1)
  };
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;

  useEffect(() => {
    wakeRef.current?.();
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!container || !canvas) return;

    const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl2', {
      alpha: true,
      premultipliedAlpha: true,
      antialias: false,
      depth: false,
      stencil: false,
      powerPreference: 'high-performance'
    });
    const shader = gl ? link(gl) : null;
    if (!gl || !shader) return;

    const reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const vao = gl.createVertexArray();
    const size = { width: 1, height: 1 };
    const pointer = { x: 0.5, y: 0.5, inside: false };
    const mouse = { x: 0.5, y: 0.5, presence: 0 };
    const pulses = [];
    const pulseData = new Float32Array(MAX_PULSES * 4);
    let flow = 0;
    let hue = 0;
    let opening = settingsRef.current.intro && !reduced && generation === 0 ? 0 : 1;
    let raf = 0;
    let last = 0;
    let visible = true;
    let pageVisible = document.visibilityState !== 'hidden';
    let lost = false;

    const resize = () => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
      const cssWidth = Math.max(1, rect.width);
      const cssHeight = Math.max(1, rect.height);
      const ratio = clamp(0.35 + 0.04 * s.noiseFrequency * s.scale, 0.5, 1);
      const width = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssWidth * ratio));
      const height = Math.max(1, Math.round(cssHeight * ratio));
      if (canvas.width !== width || canvas.height !== height) {
        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
      }
      size.width = cssWidth;
      size.height = cssHeight;
    };

    const frame = now => {
      raf = 0;
      if (lost) return;
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const dt = clamp((now - last) / 1000 || 0, 0, 0.05);
      last = Math.max(last, now);
      const pace = reduced ? 0.35 : 1;
      flow += dt * s.speed * pace;
      hue += dt * s.speed * s.colorSpeed * pace;
      if (opening < 1) opening = Math.min(1, opening + dt / 2);

      const tracking = s.mouse && pointer.inside;
      const follow = 1 - Math.exp(-dt / 0.325);
      mouse.x += ((tracking ? pointer.x : 0.5) - mouse.x) * follow;
      mouse.y += ((tracking ? pointer.y : 0.5) - mouse.y) * follow;
      mouse.presence += ((tracking ? 1 : 0) - mouse.presence) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / (tracking ? 0.45 : 0.8)));

      for (let index = pulses.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
        pulses[index].age += dt;
        if (pulses[index].age >= 2.6) pulses.splice(index, 1);
      }
      pulseData.fill(0);
      pulses.forEach((pulse, index) => {
        pulseData[index * 4] = pulse.x;
        pulseData[index * 4 + 1] = pulse.y;
        pulseData[index * 4 + 2] = smoother(pulse.age / 0.18) * (1 - smoother((pulse.age - 0.18) / 2.42));
        pulseData[index * 4 + 3] = pulse.age;
      });

      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - opening, 3);
      const aspect = size.width / size.height;
      const strength = Math.min(1, s.influence * 4) * mouse.presence;
      gl.viewport(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      gl.clearColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
      gl.clear(gl.COLOR_BUFFER_BIT);
      gl.useProgram(shader.program);
      gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
      const u = shader.uniforms;
      gl.uniform2f(u.uResolution, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uFlow, flow);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uHue, hue);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uScale, s.scale);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uBrightness, s.brightness);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uColor1, s.color1[0], s.color1[1], s.color1[2]);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uColor2, s.color2[0], s.color2[1], s.color2[2]);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uNoiseFreq, s.noiseFrequency);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uNoiseAmp, s.noiseAmplitude);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uBandHeight, s.bandHeight);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uBandSpread, s.bandSpread);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uOctaveDecay, s.octaveDecay);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uLayerOffset, s.layerOffset);
      gl.uniform2f(
        u.uShift,
        (mouse.x - 0.5) * s.influence * (s.mouse ? 1 : 0),
        (mouse.y - 0.5) * s.influence * (s.mouse ? 1 : 0)
      );
      gl.uniform4f(u.uCursor, mouse.x * aspect, mouse.y, strength, 0);
      gl.uniform4fv(u.uPulses, pulseData);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uSpectrum, s.spectrum);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uTilt, s.tilt);
      gl.uniform3f(u.uIntro, smoother(opening / 0.7), 1 + 3 * (1 - eased) * (1 - eased), 0.25 + 0.75 * eased);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uLightMode, s.light ? 1 : 0);
      gl.uniform1f(u.uOpacity, s.opacity);
      gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 3);

      const settling =
        Math.abs(mouse.x - (tracking ? pointer.x : 0.5)) > 1e-4 ||
        Math.abs(mouse.y - (tracking ? pointer.y : 0.5)) > 1e-4 ||
        Math.abs((tracking ? 1 : 0) - mouse.presence) > 1e-3 ||
        pulses.length > 0;
      const busy = s.speed > 0 || opening < 1 || settling;
      if (busy && visible && pageVisible) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };

    const wake = () => {
      if (raf || lost || !visible || !pageVisible) return;
      last = performance.now();
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
    wakeRef.current = () => {
      resize();
      wake();
    };

    const locate = event => {
      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
      return {
        x: (event.clientX - rect.left) / Math.max(1, rect.width),
        y: 1 - (event.clientY - rect.top) / Math.max(1, rect.height),
        inside:
          event.clientX >= rect.left &&
          event.clientX <= rect.right &&
          event.clientY >= rect.top &&
          event.clientY <= rect.bottom
      };
    };

    const onPointerMove = event => {
      const spot = locate(event);
      pointer.x = spot.x;
      pointer.y = spot.y;
      pointer.inside = spot.inside;
      wake();
    };
    const onPointerDown = event => {
      const s = settingsRef.current;
      const spot = locate(event);
      if (!spot.inside || !s.clickPulse || reduced) return;
      pulses.push({ x: spot.x * (size.width / size.height), y: spot.y, age: 0 });
      if (pulses.length > MAX_PULSES) pulses.shift();
      wake();
    };
    const onLeave = () => {
      pointer.inside = false;
      wake();
    };
    const onPointerUp = event => {
      if (event.pointerType !== 'mouse') onLeave();
    };
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerUp, { passive: true });
    document.documentElement.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);

    const onLost = event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      lost = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = 0;
    };
    const onRestored = () => setGeneration(value => value + 1);
    canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
    canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      resize();
      wake();
    });
    resizeObserver.observe(container);

    const intersection = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      visible = entries.some(entry => entry.isIntersecting);
      wake();
    });
    intersection.observe(container);

    const onVisibility = () => {
      pageVisible = document.visibilityState !== 'hidden';
      wake();
    };
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);

    resize();
    wake();

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      wakeRef.current = null;
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      intersection.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);
      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);
      window.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onPointerUp);
      document.documentElement.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
      canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
      canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);
      if (!gl.isContextLost()) {
        gl.deleteVertexArray(vao);
        gl.deleteProgram(shader.program);
      }
    };
  }, [generation]);

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className={`soft-aurora${className ? ` ${className}` : ''}`} style={style}>
      <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="soft-aurora__canvas" aria-hidden="true" />
    </div>
  );
};

export default SoftAurora;








.soft-aurora {
  position: relative;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.soft-aurora__canvas {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
}








Reflective Card








import ReflectiveCard from './ReflectiveCard';

<ReflectiveCard
  name="Alexander Doe"
  role="Senior Developer"
  videoSrc="/videos/reflection.mp4"
  webcam={false}
  tint="#ffffff"
  metalness={0.75}
  roughness={0}
  blurStrength={0.5}
  shine={0}
  glare={0.1}
  grayscale={0.25}
  warp={0.3}
  warpScale={1}
  bevel={2}
  tiltStrength={12}
  radius={22}
  enableTilt
  intro
/>










'use client';

import { memo, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { HugeiconsIcon } from '@hugeicons/react';
import { CheckmarkBadge01Icon, FingerPrintIcon } from '@hugeicons/core-free-icons';

import './ReflectiveCard.css';

const THEMES = {
  dark: {
    '--rc-base': '#1d1c22',
    '--rc-shadow': '0 22px 44px -20px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.8), 0 6px 16px -8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)',
    '--rc-ink': '#f4f4f5',
    '--rc-muted': 'rgba(244, 244, 245, 0.6)',
    '--rc-tile': 'linear-gradient(180deg, #3a3644 0%, #27242f 100%)',
    '--rc-tile-edge': 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07)',
    '--rc-tile-highlight': 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)',
    '--rc-tile-shadow': '0 4px 10px -4px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7)',
    '--rc-rim': 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.07)',
    '--rc-rim-highlight': 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1)'
  },
  light: {
    '--rc-base': '#e7e7ea',
    '--rc-shadow': '0 22px 44px -22px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.3), 0 6px 16px -10px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.16)',
    '--rc-ink': '#18181b',
    '--rc-muted': 'rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.6)',
    '--rc-tile': 'linear-gradient(180deg, #ffffff 0%, #f6f6f8 100%)',
    '--rc-tile-edge': 'rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.08)',
    '--rc-tile-highlight': 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 1)',
    '--rc-tile-shadow': '0 4px 10px -5px rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.25)',
    '--rc-rim': 'rgba(24, 24, 27, 0.08)',
    '--rc-rim-highlight': 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8)'
  }
};

const VIEW_DISTANCE = 900;
const OVERSCAN = 1.2;
const INTRO_MS = 1400;
const FADE_SECONDS = 0.35;

const VERTEX = `#version 300 es
in vec2 aPosition;
out vec2 vUv;
void main() {
  vUv = aPosition * 0.5 + 0.5;
  gl_Position = vec4(aPosition, 0.0, 1.0);
}`;

const CAPTURE = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
in vec2 vUv;
out vec4 fragColor;
uniform sampler2D uVideo;
uniform sampler2D uCam;
uniform vec4 uVideoMap;
uniform vec4 uCamMap;
uniform vec2 uWeights;
uniform vec2 uLod;
float level(sampler2D source) {
  vec3 mean = pow(textureLod(source, vec2(0.5), 14.0).rgb, vec3(2.2));
  return pow(dot(mean, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)), 1.0 / 2.2);
}
void main() {
  vec3 color = vec3(0.0);
  float mean = 0.0;
  if (uWeights.x > 0.0) {
    color += textureLod(uVideo, vUv * uVideoMap.xy + uVideoMap.zw, uLod.x).rgb * uWeights.x;
    mean += level(uVideo) * uWeights.x;
  }
  if (uWeights.y > 0.0) {
    color += textureLod(uCam, vUv * uCamMap.xy + uCamMap.zw, uLod.y).rgb * uWeights.y;
    mean += level(uCam) * uWeights.y;
  }
  fragColor = vec4(color, mean);
}`;

const BLUR = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
in vec2 vUv;
out vec4 fragColor;
uniform sampler2D uTexture;
uniform vec2 uStep;
uniform float uSigma;
void main() {
  vec4 sum = texture(uTexture, vUv);
  if (uSigma < 0.35) {
    fragColor = sum;
    return;
  }
  float falloff = -0.5 / (uSigma * uSigma);
  int radius = int(min(ceil(uSigma * 2.8), 40.0));
  float total = 1.0;
  for (int i = 1; i <= 40; i++) {
    if (i > radius) break;
    float offset = float(i);
    float weight = exp(offset * offset * falloff);
    sum += (texture(uTexture, vUv + uStep * offset) + texture(uTexture, vUv - uStep * offset)) * weight;
    total += 2.0 * weight;
  }
  fragColor = sum / total;
}`;

const PLATE = `#version 300 es
precision highp float;
in vec2 vUv;
out vec4 fragColor;

uniform sampler2D uEnv;
uniform vec2 uEnvSize;
uniform vec2 uSize;
uniform float uRadius;
uniform float uScale;
uniform mat3 uRotate;
uniform float uCapture;
uniform float uBlur;
uniform float uRough;
uniform float uMetal;
uniform vec3 uTint;
uniform float uGray;
uniform float uWarp;
uniform float uWarpScale;
uniform float uShine;
uniform float uBevel;
uniform float uGlare;
uniform vec2 uPointer;
uniform float uTheme;

const float VIEW = ${VIEW_DISTANCE.toFixed(1)};
const float OVERSCAN = ${OVERSCAN.toFixed(2)};
const float CONTENT = 46.0;

float hash(vec2 p) {
  vec3 p3 = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * 0.1031);
  p3 += dot(p3, p3.yzx + 33.33);
  return fract((p3.x + p3.y) * p3.z);
}

vec2 hash2(vec2 p) {
  vec3 p3 = fract(vec3(p.xyx) * vec3(0.1031, 0.103, 0.0973));
  p3 += dot(p3, p3.yzx + 33.33);
  return fract((p3.xx + p3.yz) * p3.zy) * 2.0 - 1.0;
}

vec3 gradientNoise(vec2 p) {
  vec2 i = floor(p);
  vec2 f = fract(p);
  vec2 u = f * f * f * (f * (f * 6.0 - 15.0) + 10.0);
  vec2 du = 30.0 * f * f * (f * (f - 2.0) + 1.0);
  vec2 ga = hash2(i);
  vec2 gb = hash2(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0));
  vec2 gc = hash2(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0));
  vec2 gd = hash2(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0));
  float va = dot(ga, f);
  float vb = dot(gb, f - vec2(1.0, 0.0));
  float vc = dot(gc, f - vec2(0.0, 1.0));
  float vd = dot(gd, f - vec2(1.0, 1.0));
  float k = va - vb - vc + vd;
  float value = va + u.x * (vb - va) + u.y * (vc - va) + u.x * u.y * k;
  vec2 slope = ga + u.x * (gb - ga) + u.y * (gc - ga) + u.x * u.y * (ga - gb - gc + gd) + du * (u.yx * k + vec2(vb, vc) - va);
  return vec3(value, slope);
}

float valueNoise(vec2 p) {
  vec2 i = floor(p);
  vec2 f = fract(p);
  vec2 u = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
  float a = hash(i);
  float b = hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0));
  float c = hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0));
  float d = hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0));
  return mix(mix(a, b, u.x), mix(c, d, u.x), u.y);
}

float erf(float x) {
  float x2 = x * x;
  return sign(x) * sqrt(1.0 - exp(-x2 * (1.2732395 + 0.147 * x2) / (1.0 + 0.147 * x2)));
}

float band(float x, float halfWidth, float sigma) {
  float s = 1.4142136 * max(sigma, 0.0001);
  return 0.5 * (erf((x + halfWidth) / s) - erf((x - halfWidth) / s));
}

float strip(vec2 q, vec2 center, vec2 dir, float halfWidth, vec2 sigma) {
  vec2 normal = vec2(-dir.y, dir.x);
  return band(dot(q - center, normal), halfWidth, length(sigma * normal));
}

const vec2 SWEEP = vec2(0.5, 0.86);
const vec3 LUMA = vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722);

float softbox(vec2 q, vec2 sigma) {
  float key = strip(q, vec2(-0.1, -0.16), normalize(SWEEP), 0.022, sigma + 0.018);
  float top = band(q.y + 0.6, 0.12, sigma.y + 0.05);
  float side = band(q.x - 0.46, 0.05, sigma.x + 0.05);
  return key + top * 0.8 + side * 0.35;
}

float room(vec2 q, vec2 sigma) {
  float fall = smoothstep(-0.55, 0.5, q.y);
  float flag = strip(q, vec2(0.24, -0.02), normalize(SWEEP), 0.09, sigma + 0.08);
  return mix(0.85, 0.12, fall) * (1.0 - 0.6 * flag);
}

vec4 sampleEnv(vec2 uv) {
  vec2 st = uv * uEnvSize - 0.5;
  vec2 i = floor(st);
  vec2 f = st - i;
  vec2 f2 = f * f;
  vec2 f3 = f2 * f;
  vec2 w0 = (1.0 - 3.0 * f + 3.0 * f2 - f3) / 6.0;
  vec2 w1 = (4.0 - 6.0 * f2 + 3.0 * f3) / 6.0;
  vec2 w2 = (1.0 + 3.0 * f + 3.0 * f2 - 3.0 * f3) / 6.0;
  vec2 w3 = f3 / 6.0;
  vec2 g0 = w0 + w1;
  vec2 g1 = w2 + w3;
  vec2 c0 = (i + 0.5 + w1 / g0 - 1.0) / uEnvSize;
  vec2 c1 = (i + 0.5 + w3 / g1 + 1.0) / uEnvSize;
  vec4 a = texture(uEnv, c0) * g0.x + texture(uEnv, vec2(c1.x, c0.y)) * g1.x;
  vec4 b = texture(uEnv, vec2(c0.x, c1.y)) * g0.x + texture(uEnv, c1) * g1.x;
  return a * g0.y + b * g1.y;
}

vec3 toLinear(vec3 c) {
  return pow(max(c, 0.0), vec3(2.2));
}

void main() {
  vec2 p = vec2(vUv.x - 0.5, 0.5 - vUv.y) * uSize;
  vec2 halfSize = uSize * 0.5;
  float r = min(uRadius, min(halfSize.x, halfSize.y));
  vec2 corner = abs(p) - halfSize + r;
  vec2 side = vec2(p.x >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0, p.y >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : -1.0);
  float d;
  vec2 outward;
  if (corner.x > 0.0 && corner.y > 0.0) {
    d = length(corner) - r;
    outward = normalize(corner) * side;
  } else if (corner.x > corner.y) {
    d = corner.x - r;
    outward = vec2(side.x, 0.0);
  } else {
    d = corner.y - r;
    outward = vec2(0.0, side.y);
  }
  float coverage = clamp(0.5 - d * uScale, 0.0, 1.0);

  float chamferWidth = min(uBevel, max(r, 1.0));
  float chamfer = chamferWidth > 0.01 ? 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, chamferWidth, -d) : 0.0;
  vec2 slope = outward * chamfer;

  vec2 wp = p / (220.0 * uWarpScale);
  vec3 w1 = gradientNoise(wp);
  vec3 w2 = gradientNoise(wp * 2.07 + 13.7);
  vec2 ripple = (w1.yz + w2.yz * 0.5) * uWarp;
  slope += ripple * 0.008;

  float grain = valueNoise(vec2(p.x * 0.008, p.y * 0.55)) * 0.45 + valueNoise(vec2(p.x * 0.019 + 3.7, p.y * 0.23)) * 0.35 + valueNoise(vec2(p.x * 0.004 + 9.2, p.y * 0.09)) * 0.2;
  float brush = uRough * uRough;
  slope.y += (grain - 0.5) * brush * 0.004;

  vec3 normal = uRotate * normalize(vec3(slope, 1.0));
  vec3 position = uRotate * vec3(p, 0.0);
  vec3 view = normalize(position - vec3(0.0, 0.0, VIEW));
  vec3 bounce = reflect(view, normal);
  vec2 q = bounce.xy / max(bounce.z, 0.2);

  vec2 footprint = fwidth(q);
  vec2 sigma = sqrt(vec2(uBlur / VIEW) * vec2(uBlur / VIEW) + footprint * footprint * 0.5);
  sigma += vec2(0.004, 0.05) * uRough;

  vec2 shift = clamp((q - p / VIEW) * CONTENT + ripple * 10.0, -uSize, uSize);
  vec2 cp = (p + shift) / (uSize * OVERSCAN);
  vec4 reflected = uCapture > 0.0 ? sampleEnv(vec2(0.5 + cp.x, 0.5 - cp.y)) : vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
  vec3 captured = toLinear(reflected.rgb) * (0.5 / max(pow(reflected.a, 2.2), 0.02));
  captured = mix(vec3(dot(captured, LUMA)), captured, 1.0 - uGray);
  captured = pow(max(captured * 1.2, 0.0), vec3(mix(2.0, 1.5, uTheme)));

  vec3 scene = mix(vec3(room(q, sigma)), captured, uCapture * (1.0 - chamfer) * mix(1.0, 0.6, uTheme));
  scene *= 1.0 + (grain - 0.5) * brush * 0.3;

  vec2 toPointer = (uPointer - p) / (120.0 + uBlur * 4.0);
  vec2 spread = toPointer * vec2(1.0, 1.0 / (1.0 + 2.5 * uRough));
  float reach = dot(spread, spread);
  float glint = max(1.0 + 3.0 * dot(slope, toPointer), 0.0);
  float glare = (exp(-reach * 1.6) + exp(-reach * 0.35) * 0.15) * glint * uGlare * (1.0 + 0.8 * (grain - 0.5) * brush);
  float highlight = (softbox(q, sigma) * uShine + glare) * mix(0.4, 1.0, uMetal);

  vec3 dark = vec3(0.005, 0.005, 0.006) + uTint * (scene * 0.05 * uMetal + highlight * 0.36);
  vec3 light = uTint * (vec3(0.83, 0.83, 0.85) * (1.0 - 0.45 * uMetal * (1.0 - clamp(scene, 0.0, 1.0))) + highlight * 0.35);
  vec3 color = mix(dark, light, uTheme);
  color = min(color, 0.7) + 0.3 * (1.0 - exp(-max(color - 0.7, 0.0) / 0.3));
  color = pow(clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0), vec3(1.0 / 2.2));

  color += (hash(gl_FragCoord.xy) - 0.5) / 255.0;
  color = clamp(color, 0.0, 1.0);
  fragColor = vec4(color * coverage, coverage);
}`;

const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

const toPx = value => {
  if (typeof value === 'number') return `${value}px`;
  return value;
};

const parseColor = value => {
  const text = String(value || '').trim();
  const hex = /^#?([0-9a-f]{3}|[0-9a-f]{6})$/i.exec(text);
  if (hex) {
    const full = hex[1].length === 3 ? hex[1].replace(/./g, digit => digit + digit) : hex[1];
    return [0, 2, 4].map(index => parseInt(full.slice(index, index + 2), 16) / 255);
  }
  const rgb = /^rgba?\(\s*([\d.]+)[\s,]+([\d.]+)[\s,]+([\d.]+)/i.exec(text);
  if (rgb) return [rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3]].map(channel => clamp(parseFloat(channel) / 255, 0, 1));
  return [1, 1, 1];
};

const compile = (gl, type, source) => {
  const shader = gl.createShader(type);
  if (!shader) return null;
  gl.shaderSource(shader, source);
  gl.compileShader(shader);
  if (!gl.getShaderParameter(shader, gl.COMPILE_STATUS)) {
    console.error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(shader));
    gl.deleteShader(shader);
    return null;
  }
  return shader;
};

const link = (gl, fragmentSource) => {
  const vertex = compile(gl, gl.VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX);
  const fragment = compile(gl, gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER, fragmentSource);
  const program = vertex && fragment ? gl.createProgram() : null;
  if (!vertex || !fragment || !program) {
    if (vertex) gl.deleteShader(vertex);
    if (fragment) gl.deleteShader(fragment);
    return null;
  }
  gl.attachShader(program, vertex);
  gl.attachShader(program, fragment);
  gl.bindAttribLocation(program, 0, 'aPosition');
  gl.linkProgram(program);
  gl.deleteShader(vertex);
  gl.deleteShader(fragment);
  if (!gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.LINK_STATUS)) {
    console.error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(program));
    gl.deleteProgram(program);
    return null;
  }
  const uniforms = {};
  const count = gl.getProgramParameter(program, gl.ACTIVE_UNIFORMS);
  for (let index = 0; index < count; index++) {
    const info = gl.getActiveUniform(program, index);
    if (info) uniforms[info.name] = gl.getUniformLocation(program, info.name);
  }
  return { program, uniforms };
};

const coverMap = (width, height, aspect, mirror) => {
  const ratio = width / Math.max(1, height);
  const sx = ratio > aspect ? aspect / ratio : 1;
  const sy = ratio > aspect ? 1 : ratio / aspect;
  const u = mirror ? -sx : sx;
  return [u, -sy, 0.5 - u * 0.5, 0.5 + sy * 0.5];
};

const lodFor = (width, height, envWidth, aspect) => {
  const ratio = width / Math.max(1, height);
  const visible = width * (ratio > aspect ? aspect / ratio : 1);
  return Math.max(0, Math.log2(Math.max(1, visible / Math.max(1, envWidth))) - 0.5);
};

const createRenderer = canvas => {
  const gl = canvas.getContext('webgl2', {
    alpha: true,
    premultipliedAlpha: true,
    antialias: false,
    depth: false,
    stencil: false,
    powerPreference: 'default'
  });
  if (!gl) return null;
  const captureProgram = link(gl, CAPTURE);
  const blurProgram = link(gl, BLUR);
  const plateProgram = link(gl, PLATE);
  if (!captureProgram || !blurProgram || !plateProgram) {
    for (const item of [captureProgram, blurProgram, plateProgram]) {
      if (item) gl.deleteProgram(item.program);
    }
    return null;
  }

  const createTexture = (mipmaps, wrap) => {
    const texture = gl.createTexture();
    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);
    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, mipmaps ? gl.LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR : gl.LINEAR);
    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, gl.LINEAR);
    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S, wrap);
    gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D, gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T, wrap);
    gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, 1, 1, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, new Uint8Array([0, 0, 0, 255]));
    return texture;
  };

  const createTarget = () => {
    const texture = createTexture(false, gl.MIRRORED_REPEAT);
    const framebuffer = gl.createFramebuffer();
    gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, framebuffer);
    gl.framebufferTexture2D(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, gl.COLOR_ATTACHMENT0, gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture, 0);
    gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, null);
    return { texture, framebuffer, width: 1, height: 1 };
  };

  const allocate = (target, width, height) => {
    if (target.width === width && target.height === height) return;
    target.width = width;
    target.height = height;
    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, target.texture);
    gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, width, height, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, null);
  };

  const vao = gl.createVertexArray();
  gl.bindVertexArray(vao);
  const buffer = gl.createBuffer();
  gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, buffer);
  gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER, new Float32Array([-1, -1, 3, -1, -1, 3]), gl.STATIC_DRAW);
  gl.enableVertexAttribArray(0);
  gl.vertexAttribPointer(0, 2, gl.FLOAT, false, 0, 0);
  gl.disable(gl.BLEND);
  gl.disable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);

  const textures = { video: createTexture(true, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE), cam: createTexture(true, gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE) };
  const capture = createTarget();
  const blurX = createTarget();
  const blurY = createTarget();
  const rotation = new Float32Array(9);

  const pass = (target, program) => {
    gl.bindFramebuffer(gl.FRAMEBUFFER, target ? target.framebuffer : null);
    gl.viewport(0, 0, target ? target.width : canvas.width, target ? target.height : canvas.height);
    gl.useProgram(program.program);
    return program.uniforms;
  };

  const bind = (unit, texture) => {
    gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0 + unit);
    gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, texture);
  };

  const upload = (key, element) => {
    try {
      gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D, textures[key]);
      gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_FLIP_Y_WEBGL, false);
      gl.pixelStorei(gl.UNPACK_PREMULTIPLY_ALPHA_WEBGL, false);
      gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D, 0, gl.RGBA, gl.RGBA, gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE, element);
      gl.generateMipmap(gl.TEXTURE_2D);
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  const renderEnv = ({ width, height, scale, blur, roughness, video, cam }) => {
    const envScale = clamp(2.5 / Math.max(blur, 0.01), 0.12, scale);
    const envWidth = Math.max(8, Math.ceil(width * OVERSCAN * envScale));
    const envHeight = Math.max(8, Math.ceil(height * OVERSCAN * envScale));
    allocate(capture, envWidth, envHeight);
    allocate(blurX, envWidth, envHeight);
    allocate(blurY, envWidth, envHeight);
    const aspect = width / Math.max(1, height);
    const total = 1 - (1 - video.mix) * (1 - cam.mix);
    const camWeight = total > 0 ? cam.mix / total : 0;

    let u = pass(capture, captureProgram);
    bind(0, textures.video);
    bind(1, textures.cam);
    gl.uniform1i(u.uVideo, 0);
    gl.uniform1i(u.uCam, 1);
    gl.uniform4fv(u.uVideoMap, video.ready ? coverMap(video.width, video.height, aspect, false) : [1, 1, 0, 0]);
    gl.uniform4fv(u.uCamMap, cam.ready ? coverMap(cam.width, cam.height, aspect, true) : [1, 1, 0, 0]);
    gl.uniform2f(u.uWeights, video.ready && total > 0 ? 1 - camWeight : 0, cam.ready ? camWeight : 0);
    gl.uniform2f(
      u.uLod,
      video.ready ? lodFor(video.width, video.height, envWidth, aspect) : 0,
      cam.ready ? lodFor(cam.width, cam.height, envWidth, aspect) : 0
    );
    gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 3);

    u = pass(blurX, blurProgram);
    bind(0, capture.texture);
    gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, 0);
    gl.uniform2f(u.uStep, 1 / envWidth, 0);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uSigma, blur * envScale);
    gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 3);

    u = pass(blurY, blurProgram);
    bind(0, blurX.texture);
    gl.uniform1i(u.uTexture, 0);
    gl.uniform2f(u.uStep, 0, 1 / envHeight);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uSigma, (blur * (1 + 1.5 * roughness) + 10 * roughness) * envScale);
    gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 3);
  };

  const renderPlate = ({ width, height, rotateX, rotateY, capture: amount, glare, pointerX, pointerY, settings }) => {
    const cx = Math.cos(rotateX);
    const sx = Math.sin(rotateX);
    const cy = Math.cos(rotateY);
    const sy = Math.sin(rotateY);
    rotation.set([cy, sx * sy, -cx * sy, 0, cx, sx, sy, -sx * cy, cx * cy]);

    const u = pass(null, plateProgram);
    bind(0, blurY.texture);
    gl.uniform1i(u.uEnv, 0);
    gl.uniform2f(u.uEnvSize, blurY.width, blurY.height);
    gl.uniform2f(u.uSize, width, height);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uRadius, settings.radius);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uScale, canvas.width / Math.max(1, width));
    gl.uniformMatrix3fv(u.uRotate, false, rotation);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uCapture, amount);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uBlur, settings.blurStrength);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uRough, settings.roughness);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uMetal, settings.metalness);
    gl.uniform3fv(u.uTint, settings.tint);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uGray, settings.grayscale);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uWarp, settings.warp);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uWarpScale, settings.warpScale);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uShine, settings.shine);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uBevel, settings.bevel);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uGlare, glare);
    gl.uniform2f(u.uPointer, pointerX, pointerY);
    gl.uniform1f(u.uTheme, settings.theme);
    gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLES, 0, 3);
  };

  const dispose = () => {
    if (gl.isContextLost()) return;
    for (const target of [capture, blurX, blurY]) {
      gl.deleteFramebuffer(target.framebuffer);
      gl.deleteTexture(target.texture);
    }
    gl.deleteTexture(textures.video);
    gl.deleteTexture(textures.cam);
    gl.deleteBuffer(buffer);
    gl.deleteVertexArray(vao);
    for (const item of [captureProgram, blurProgram, plateProgram]) gl.deleteProgram(item.program);
  };

  return { upload, renderEnv, renderPlate, dispose };
};

const ReflectiveCard = ({
  name = 'Alexander Doe',
  role = 'Senior Developer',
  badge = 'Verified',
  status = 'Active',
  statusColor = '#22c55e',
  idLabel = 'ID number',
  idValue = '8901 2345 6789',
  children,
  theme = 'dark',
  videoSrc,
  webcam = false,
  onWebcamError,
  tint = '#ffffff',
  metalness = 1,
  roughness = 0.3,
  blurStrength = 6,
  grayscale = 1,
  warp = 0.6,
  warpScale = 1,
  shine = 1,
  bevel = 3,
  glare = 0.5,
  enableTilt = true,
  tiltStrength = 12,
  intro = true,
  width = 320,
  height = 500,
  radius = 24,
  className = '',
  style
}) => {
  const rootRef = useRef(null);
  const bodyRef = useRef(null);
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const videoRef = useRef(null);
  const camRef = useRef(null);
  const wakeRef = useRef(null);
  const errorRef = useRef(onWebcamError);
  const [generation, setGeneration] = useState(0);

  const palette = theme === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark';
  const settings = {
    tint: parseColor(tint).map(channel => Math.pow(channel, 2.2)),
    metalness: clamp(metalness, 0, 1),
    roughness: clamp(roughness, 0, 1),
    blurStrength: clamp(blurStrength, 0, 40),
    grayscale: clamp(grayscale, 0, 1),
    warp: clamp(warp, 0, 2),
    warpScale: clamp(warpScale, 0.2, 4),
    shine: clamp(shine, 0, 3),
    bevel: clamp(bevel, 0, 16),
    glare: clamp(glare, 0, 1),
    enableTilt,
    tiltStrength: clamp(tiltStrength, 0, 30),
    radius: Math.max(0, radius),
    theme: palette === 'light' ? 1 : 0,
    intro
  };
  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);
  settingsRef.current = settings;
  errorRef.current = onWebcamError;

  useEffect(() => {
    wakeRef.current?.();
  });

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const root = rootRef.current;
    if (!root || !settingsRef.current.intro) return;
    if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;
    root.style.setProperty('--rc-presence', '0');
    root.style.setProperty('--rc-lift', '14px');
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = rootRef.current;
    const body = bodyRef.current;
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const videoElement = videoRef.current;
    const camElement = camRef.current;
    if (!root || !body || !canvas || !videoElement || !camElement) return;

    const reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const renderer = createRenderer(canvas);
    const x = { p: 0, v: 0, target: 0 };
    const y = { p: 0, v: 0, target: 0 };
    const light = { p: 0, target: 0 };
    const pointer = { x: 0, y: 0 };
    const size = { width: body.offsetWidth, height: body.offsetHeight, scale: 1 };
    const makeSource = element => ({
      element,
      src: '',
      time: -1,
      ready: false,
      failed: false,
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      mix: 0
    });
    const sources = { video: makeSource(videoElement), cam: makeSource(camElement) };
    let presence = 1;
    let presenceStart = 0;
    let slowUntil = 0;
    let touchId = null;
    let raf = 0;
    let last = 0;
    let visible = true;
    let pageVisible = document.visibilityState !== 'hidden';
    let lost = false;
    let envKey = '';
    let envFresh = false;

    const write = () => {
      const { enableTilt: tiltOn, tiltStrength: strength } = settingsRef.current;
      const angle = tiltOn && !reduced ? strength : 0;
      const style = root.style;
      style.setProperty('--rc-rotate-x', `${(y.p * angle).toFixed(3)}deg`);
      style.setProperty('--rc-rotate-y', `${(-x.p * angle).toFixed(3)}deg`);
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - presence, 3);
      style.setProperty('--rc-presence', eased.toFixed(4));
      style.setProperty('--rc-lift', `${((1 - eased) * 14).toFixed(2)}px`);
    };

    const refresh = (key, source) => {
      const element = source.element;
      const src = element.srcObject ? 'stream' : element.currentSrc;
      if (src !== source.src) {
        source.src = src;
        source.failed = false;
        source.time = -1;
      }
      if (!src) {
        source.ready = false;
        return false;
      }
      if (element.error) {
        source.failed = true;
        return false;
      }
      if (element.readyState < 2 || !element.videoWidth) return false;
      const time = element.currentTime;
      if (source.ready && time === source.time) return false;
      if (!renderer || !renderer.upload(key, element)) {
        source.failed = true;
        return false;
      }
      source.time = time;
      source.width = element.videoWidth;
      source.height = element.videoHeight;
      source.ready = true;
      return true;
    };

    const fade = (source, dt) => {
      const target = source.ready && !source.failed && source.src ? 1 : 0;
      const before = source.mix;
      source.mix += (target - source.mix) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / FADE_SECONDS));
      if (Math.abs(target - source.mix) < 0.002) source.mix = target;
      return source.mix !== before;
    };

    const resize = () => {
      size.width = body.offsetWidth;
      size.height = body.offsetHeight;
      size.scale = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      const w = Math.max(1, Math.round(size.width * size.scale));
      const h = Math.max(1, Math.round(size.height * size.scale));
      if (canvas.width !== w || canvas.height !== h) {
        canvas.width = w;
        canvas.height = h;
      }
      envFresh = true;
    };

    const frame = now => {
      raf = 0;
      if (lost) return;
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (now - last) / 1000 || 0);
      last = now;
      const slow = now < slowUntil;
      const stiffness = slow ? 24 : 120;
      const damping = slow ? 9 : 16;
      const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dt / (1 / 120)));
      const h = dt / steps;
      for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
        for (const axis of [x, y]) {
          axis.v += (stiffness * (axis.target - axis.p) - damping * axis.v) * h;
          axis.p += axis.v * h;
        }
      }
      const tau = light.target > light.p ? 0.18 : slow ? 0.9 : 0.45;
      light.p += (light.target - light.p) * (1 - Math.exp(-dt / tau));
      if (presence < 1) presence = clamp((now - presenceStart) / 650, 0, 1);

      const moving =
        Math.abs(x.target - x.p) > 0.0005 ||
        Math.abs(y.target - y.p) > 0.0005 ||
        Math.abs(x.v) > 0.0005 ||
        Math.abs(y.v) > 0.0005 ||
        Math.abs(light.target - light.p) > 0.002 ||
        presence < 1;
      if (!moving) {
        x.p = x.target;
        y.p = y.target;
        x.v = 0;
        y.v = 0;
        light.p = light.target;
      }
      write();

      let playing = false;
      if (renderer) {
        if (sources.cam.mix < 1 && refresh('video', sources.video)) envFresh = true;
        if (refresh('cam', sources.cam)) envFresh = true;
        for (const source of [sources.video, sources.cam]) {
          if (source.src && !source.failed && !source.element.paused) playing = true;
          if (fade(source, dt)) envFresh = true;
          if (source.mix > 0 && source.mix < 1) playing = true;
        }
        const s = settingsRef.current;
        const key = `${s.blurStrength}|${s.roughness}|${size.width}|${size.height}|${size.scale}`;
        if (key !== envKey) {
          envKey = key;
          envFresh = true;
        }
        if (envFresh && (sources.video.mix > 0 || sources.cam.mix > 0)) {
          renderer.renderEnv({
            width: size.width,
            height: size.height,
            scale: size.scale,
            blur: s.blurStrength,
            roughness: s.roughness,
            video: sources.video,
            cam: sources.cam
          });
        }
        envFresh = false;
        const angle = (s.enableTilt && !reduced ? s.tiltStrength : 0) * (Math.PI / 180);
        renderer.renderPlate({
          width: size.width,
          height: size.height,
          rotateX: y.p * angle,
          rotateY: -x.p * angle,
          capture: 1 - (1 - sources.video.mix) * (1 - sources.cam.mix),
          glare: s.glare * light.p,
          pointerX: pointer.x,
          pointerY: pointer.y,
          settings: s
        });
      }

      if ((moving || playing) && visible && pageVisible) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };

    const wake = () => {
      if (raf || lost || !visible || !pageVisible) return;
      last = performance.now();
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
    wakeRef.current = wake;

    const aim = (nx, ny, on = true) => {
      x.target = clamp(nx, -1, 1);
      y.target = clamp(ny, -1, 1);
      light.target = on ? 1 : 0;
      slowUntil = 0;
      wake();
    };

    const aimAt = event => {
      const rect = root.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (!rect.width || !rect.height) return;
      const nx = ((event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
      const ny = ((event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 - 1;
      pointer.x = nx * size.width * 0.5;
      pointer.y = ny * size.height * 0.5;
      aim(nx, ny);
    };

    const release = () => aim(0, 0, false);

    const onEnter = event => {
      if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') aimAt(event);
    };
    const onMove = event => {
      if (event.pointerType === 'mouse' || event.pointerId === touchId) aimAt(event);
    };
    const onLeave = event => {
      if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') release();
    };
    const onDown = event => {
      if (event.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
      touchId = event.pointerId;
      aimAt(event);
    };
    const onUp = event => {
      if (event.pointerId !== touchId) return;
      touchId = null;
      release();
    };

    root.addEventListener('pointerenter', onEnter);
    root.addEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
    root.addEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
    root.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
    root.addEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
    root.addEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);

    const onLost = event => {
      event.preventDefault();
      lost = true;
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      raf = 0;
    };
    const onRestored = () => setGeneration(value => value + 1);
    canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
    canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);

    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {
      resize();
      wake();
    });
    resizeObserver.observe(body);
    resize();

    const intersection = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      visible = entries.some(entry => entry.isIntersecting);
      if (!reduced) {
        for (const element of [videoElement, camElement]) {
          if (visible && (element.currentSrc || element.srcObject)) element.play().catch(() => {});
          else if (!visible && element.currentSrc && !element.srcObject) element.pause();
        }
      }
      wake();
    });
    intersection.observe(root);

    const onVisibility = () => {
      pageVisible = document.visibilityState !== 'hidden';
      wake();
    };
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);

    const onMedia = () => wake();
    const mediaEvents = ['loadeddata', 'playing', 'seeked', 'emptied', 'error'];
    for (const element of [videoElement, camElement]) {
      for (const type of mediaEvents) element.addEventListener(type, onMedia);
    }

    if (settingsRef.current.intro && !reduced && generation === 0) {
      x.p = 0.62;
      y.p = -0.55;
      light.p = 1;
      pointer.x = 0.62 * size.width * 0.5;
      pointer.y = -0.55 * size.height * 0.5;
      presence = 0;
      presenceStart = performance.now();
      slowUntil = performance.now() + INTRO_MS;
    }
    write();
    wake();

    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      wakeRef.current = null;
      resizeObserver.disconnect();
      intersection.disconnect();
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
      root.removeEventListener('pointerenter', onEnter);
      root.removeEventListener('pointermove', onMove);
      root.removeEventListener('pointerleave', onLeave);
      root.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
      root.removeEventListener('pointerup', onUp);
      root.removeEventListener('pointercancel', onUp);
      canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextlost', onLost);
      canvas.removeEventListener('webglcontextrestored', onRestored);
      for (const element of [videoElement, camElement]) {
        for (const type of mediaEvents) element.removeEventListener(type, onMedia);
      }
      renderer?.dispose();
    };
  }, [generation]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const video = videoRef.current;
    if (!video) return;
    if (!videoSrc) {
      video.removeAttribute('src');
      video.load();
      wakeRef.current?.();
      return;
    }
    video.src = videoSrc;
    if (!window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) video.play().catch(() => {});
    wakeRef.current?.();
    return () => video.pause();
  }, [videoSrc]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const cam = camRef.current;
    if (!webcam || !cam) return;
    const media = navigator.mediaDevices;
    if (!media || !media.getUserMedia) {
      errorRef.current?.(new Error('Camera access is not available in this browser.'));
      return;
    }
    let stream = null;
    let cancelled = false;
    media
      .getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: 'user', width: { ideal: 640 }, height: { ideal: 480 } }, audio: false })
      .then(result => {
        if (cancelled) {
          result.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
          return;
        }
        stream = result;
        cam.srcObject = result;
        cam.play().catch(() => {});
        wakeRef.current?.();
      })
      .catch(error => {
        if (!cancelled) errorRef.current?.(error);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      stream?.getTracks().forEach(track => track.stop());
      cam.srcObject = null;
      wakeRef.current?.();
    };
  }, [webcam]);

  const corner = Math.max(0, radius);
  const cardStyle = {
    ...THEMES[palette],
    '--rc-width': toPx(width),
    '--rc-height': toPx(height),
    '--rc-radius': `${corner}px`,
    '--rc-pad': `${Math.round(clamp(corner * 0.8, 16, 22))}px`,
    '--rc-tile-radius': `${Math.round(clamp(corner * 0.5, 8, 14))}px`,
    '--rc-status': statusColor
  };

  return (
    <div ref={rootRef} className={`reflective-card ${className}`.trim()} style={{ ...cardStyle, ...style }}>
      <div ref={bodyRef} className="reflective-card__body">
        <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="reflective-card__canvas" aria-hidden="true" />
        <div className="reflective-card__content">
          {children ?? (
            <>
              {(badge || status) && (
                <div className="reflective-card__header">
                  {badge && (
                    <span className="reflective-card__badge">
                      <HugeiconsIcon icon={CheckmarkBadge01Icon} size={16} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                      {badge}
                    </span>
                  )}
                  {status && (
                    <span className="reflective-card__status">
                      <span className="reflective-card__dot" />
                      {status}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="reflective-card__identity">
                {name && <h3 className="reflective-card__name">{name}</h3>}
                {role && <p className="reflective-card__role">{role}</p>}
              </div>
              {(idLabel || idValue) && (
                <div className="reflective-card__footer">
                  <div className="reflective-card__id">
                    {idLabel && <span className="reflective-card__label">{idLabel}</span>}
                    {idValue && <span className="reflective-card__value">{idValue}</span>}
                  </div>
                  <span className="reflective-card__tile">
                    <HugeiconsIcon icon={FingerPrintIcon} size={22} strokeWidth={1.6} />
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
        <span className="reflective-card__rim" />
      </div>
      <video
        ref={videoRef}
        className="reflective-card__media"
        muted
        loop
        playsInline
        crossOrigin="anonymous"
        aria-hidden="true"
      />
      <video ref={camRef} className="reflective-card__media" muted playsInline aria-hidden="true" />
    </div>
  );
};

export default memo(ReflectiveCard);

Staggered Menu












import StaggeredMenu from './StaggeredMenu';

const menuItems = [
  { label: 'Home', ariaLabel: 'Go to home page', link: '/' },
  { label: 'About', ariaLabel: 'Learn about us', link: '/about' },
  { label: 'Services', ariaLabel: 'View our services', link: '/services' },
  { label: 'Contact', ariaLabel: 'Get in touch', link: '/contact' }
];

const socialItems = [
  { label: 'Twitter', link: 'https://twitter.com' },
  { label: 'GitHub', link: 'https://github.com' },
  { label: 'LinkedIn', link: 'https://linkedin.com' }
];

<div style={{ height: '100vh', background: '#1a1a1a' }}>
  <StaggeredMenu
    position="left"
    items={menuItems}
    socialItems={socialItems}
    displaySocials
    displayItemNumbering={true}
    menuButtonColor="#ffffff"
    openMenuButtonColor="#fff"
    changeMenuColorOnOpen={true}
    colors={['#B497CF', '#5227FF']}
    logoUrl="/path-to-your-logo.svg"
    accentColor="#2e0bbb"
    onMenuOpen={() => console.log('Menu opened')}
    onMenuClose={() => console.log('Menu closed')}
  />
</div>


















'use client';

import React, { useCallback, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import './StaggeredMenu.css';

export const StaggeredMenu = ({
  position = 'right',
  colors = ['#B497CF', '#5227FF'],
  items = [],
  socialItems = [],
  displaySocials = true,
  displayItemNumbering = true,
  className,
  logoUrl = '/src/assets/logos/reactbits-gh-white.svg',
  menuButtonColor = '#fff',
  openMenuButtonColor = '#fff',
  accentColor = '#5227FF',
  changeMenuColorOnOpen = true,
  isFixed = false,
  closeOnClickAway = true,
  onMenuOpen,
  onMenuClose
}) => {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const openRef = useRef(false);
  const panelRef = useRef(null);
  const preLayersRef = useRef(null);
  const preLayerElsRef = useRef([]);
  const plusHRef = useRef(null);
  const plusVRef = useRef(null);
  const iconRef = useRef(null);
  const textInnerRef = useRef(null);
  const textWrapRef = useRef(null);
  const [textLines, setTextLines] = useState(['Menu', 'Close']);

  const openTlRef = useRef(null);
  const closeTweenRef = useRef(null);
  const spinTweenRef = useRef(null);
  const textCycleAnimRef = useRef(null);
  const colorTweenRef = useRef(null);
  const toggleBtnRef = useRef(null);
  const busyRef = useRef(false);
  const itemEntranceTweenRef = useRef(null);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const ctx = gsap.context(() => {
      const panel = panelRef.current;
      const preContainer = preLayersRef.current;
      const plusH = plusHRef.current;
      const plusV = plusVRef.current;
      const icon = iconRef.current;
      const textInner = textInnerRef.current;
      if (!panel || !plusH || !plusV || !icon || !textInner) return;

      let preLayers = [];
      if (preContainer) {
        preLayers = Array.from(preContainer.querySelectorAll('.sm-prelayer'));
      }
      preLayerElsRef.current = preLayers;

      const offscreen = position === 'left' ? -100 : 100;
      gsap.set([panel, ...preLayers], { xPercent: offscreen, opacity: 1 });
      if (preContainer) {
        gsap.set(preContainer, { xPercent: 0, opacity: 1 });
      }
      gsap.set(plusH, { transformOrigin: '50% 50%', rotate: 0 });
      gsap.set(plusV, { transformOrigin: '50% 50%', rotate: 90 });
      gsap.set(icon, { rotate: 0, transformOrigin: '50% 50%' });
      gsap.set(textInner, { yPercent: 0 });
      if (toggleBtnRef.current) gsap.set(toggleBtnRef.current, { color: menuButtonColor });
    });
    return () => ctx.revert();
  }, [menuButtonColor, position]);

  const buildOpenTimeline = useCallback(() => {
    const panel = panelRef.current;
    const layers = preLayerElsRef.current;
    if (!panel) return null;

    openTlRef.current?.kill();
    if (closeTweenRef.current) {
      closeTweenRef.current.kill();
      closeTweenRef.current = null;
    }
    itemEntranceTweenRef.current?.kill();

    const itemEls = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll('.sm-panel-itemLabel'));
    const numberEls = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll('.sm-panel-list[data-numbering] .sm-panel-item'));
    const socialTitle = panel.querySelector('.sm-socials-title');
    const socialLinks = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll('.sm-socials-link'));

    const offscreen = position === 'left' ? -100 : 100;
    const layerStates = layers.map(el => ({ el, start: offscreen }));
    const panelStart = offscreen;

    if (itemEls.length) {
      gsap.set(itemEls, { yPercent: 140, rotate: 10 });
    }
    if (numberEls.length) {
      gsap.set(numberEls, { '--sm-num-opacity': 0 });
    }
    if (socialTitle) {
      gsap.set(socialTitle, { opacity: 0 });
    }
    if (socialLinks.length) {
      gsap.set(socialLinks, { y: 25, opacity: 0 });
    }

    const tl = gsap.timeline({ paused: true });

    layerStates.forEach((ls, i) => {
      tl.fromTo(ls.el, { xPercent: ls.start }, { xPercent: 0, duration: 0.5, ease: 'power4.out' }, i * 0.07);
    });
    const lastTime = layerStates.length ? (layerStates.length - 1) * 0.07 : 0;
    const panelInsertTime = lastTime + (layerStates.length ? 0.08 : 0);
    const panelDuration = 0.65;
    tl.fromTo(
      panel,
      { xPercent: panelStart },
      { xPercent: 0, duration: panelDuration, ease: 'power4.out' },
      panelInsertTime
    );

    if (itemEls.length) {
      const itemsStartRatio = 0.15;
      const itemsStart = panelInsertTime + panelDuration * itemsStartRatio;
      tl.to(
        itemEls,
        {
          yPercent: 0,
          rotate: 0,
          duration: 1,
          ease: 'power4.out',
          stagger: { each: 0.1, from: 'start' }
        },
        itemsStart
      );
      if (numberEls.length) {
        tl.to(
          numberEls,
          {
            duration: 0.6,
            ease: 'power2.out',
            '--sm-num-opacity': 1,
            stagger: { each: 0.08, from: 'start' }
          },
          itemsStart + 0.1
        );
      }
    }

    if (socialTitle || socialLinks.length) {
      const socialsStart = panelInsertTime + panelDuration * 0.4;
      if (socialTitle) {
        tl.to(
          socialTitle,
          {
            opacity: 1,
            duration: 0.5,
            ease: 'power2.out'
          },
          socialsStart
        );
      }
      if (socialLinks.length) {
        tl.to(
          socialLinks,
          {
            y: 0,
            opacity: 1,
            duration: 0.55,
            ease: 'power3.out',
            stagger: { each: 0.08, from: 'start' },
            onComplete: () => {
              gsap.set(socialLinks, { clearProps: 'opacity' });
            }
          },
          socialsStart + 0.04
        );
      }
    }

    openTlRef.current = tl;
    return tl;
  }, []);

  const playOpen = useCallback(() => {
    if (busyRef.current) return;
    busyRef.current = true;
    const tl = buildOpenTimeline();
    if (tl) {
      tl.eventCallback('onComplete', () => {
        busyRef.current = false;
      });
      tl.play(0);
    } else {
      busyRef.current = false;
    }
  }, [buildOpenTimeline]);

  const playClose = useCallback(() => {
    openTlRef.current?.kill();
    openTlRef.current = null;
    itemEntranceTweenRef.current?.kill();

    const panel = panelRef.current;
    const layers = preLayerElsRef.current;
    if (!panel) return;

    const all = [...layers, panel];
    closeTweenRef.current?.kill();
    const offscreen = position === 'left' ? -100 : 100;
    closeTweenRef.current = gsap.to(all, {
      xPercent: offscreen,
      duration: 0.32,
      ease: 'power3.in',
      overwrite: 'auto',
      onComplete: () => {
        const itemEls = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll('.sm-panel-itemLabel'));
        if (itemEls.length) {
          gsap.set(itemEls, { yPercent: 140, rotate: 10 });
        }
        const numberEls = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll('.sm-panel-list[data-numbering] .sm-panel-item'));
        if (numberEls.length) {
          gsap.set(numberEls, { '--sm-num-opacity': 0 });
        }
        const socialTitle = panel.querySelector('.sm-socials-title');
        const socialLinks = Array.from(panel.querySelectorAll('.sm-socials-link'));
        if (socialTitle) gsap.set(socialTitle, { opacity: 0 });
        if (socialLinks.length) gsap.set(socialLinks, { y: 25, opacity: 0 });
        busyRef.current = false;
      }
    });
  }, [position]);

  const animateIcon = useCallback(opening => {
    const icon = iconRef.current;
    if (!icon) return;
    spinTweenRef.current?.kill();
    if (opening) {
      spinTweenRef.current = gsap.to(icon, { rotate: 225, duration: 0.8, ease: 'power4.out', overwrite: 'auto' });
    } else {
      spinTweenRef.current = gsap.to(icon, { rotate: 0, duration: 0.35, ease: 'power3.inOut', overwrite: 'auto' });
    }
  }, []);

  const animateColor = useCallback(
    opening => {
      const btn = toggleBtnRef.current;
      if (!btn) return;
      colorTweenRef.current?.kill();
      if (changeMenuColorOnOpen) {
        const targetColor = opening ? openMenuButtonColor : menuButtonColor;
        colorTweenRef.current = gsap.to(btn, {
          color: targetColor,
          delay: 0.18,
          duration: 0.3,
          ease: 'power2.out'
        });
      } else {
        gsap.set(btn, { color: menuButtonColor });
      }
    },
    [openMenuButtonColor, menuButtonColor, changeMenuColorOnOpen]
  );

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (toggleBtnRef.current) {
      if (changeMenuColorOnOpen) {
        const targetColor = openRef.current ? openMenuButtonColor : menuButtonColor;
        gsap.set(toggleBtnRef.current, { color: targetColor });
      } else {
        gsap.set(toggleBtnRef.current, { color: menuButtonColor });
      }
    }
  }, [changeMenuColorOnOpen, menuButtonColor, openMenuButtonColor]);

  const animateText = useCallback(opening => {
    const inner = textInnerRef.current;
    if (!inner) return;
    textCycleAnimRef.current?.kill();

    const currentLabel = opening ? 'Menu' : 'Close';
    const targetLabel = opening ? 'Close' : 'Menu';
    const cycles = 3;
    const seq = [currentLabel];
    let last = currentLabel;
    for (let i = 0; i < cycles; i++) {
      last = last === 'Menu' ? 'Close' : 'Menu';
      seq.push(last);
    }
    if (last !== targetLabel) seq.push(targetLabel);
    seq.push(targetLabel);
    setTextLines(seq);

    gsap.set(inner, { yPercent: 0 });
    const lineCount = seq.length;
    const finalShift = ((lineCount - 1) / lineCount) * 100;
    textCycleAnimRef.current = gsap.to(inner, {
      yPercent: -finalShift,
      duration: 0.5 + lineCount * 0.07,
      ease: 'power4.out'
    });
  }, []);

  const toggleMenu = useCallback(() => {
    const target = !openRef.current;
    openRef.current = target;
    setOpen(target);
    if (target) {
      onMenuOpen?.();
      playOpen();
    } else {
      onMenuClose?.();
      playClose();
    }
    animateIcon(target);
    animateColor(target);
    animateText(target);
  }, [playOpen, playClose, animateIcon, animateColor, animateText, onMenuOpen, onMenuClose]);

  const closeMenu = useCallback(() => {
    if (openRef.current) {
      openRef.current = false;
      setOpen(false);
      onMenuClose?.();
      playClose();
      animateIcon(false);
      animateColor(false);
      animateText(false);
    }
  }, [playClose, animateIcon, animateColor, animateText, onMenuClose]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!closeOnClickAway || !open) return;

    const handleClickOutside = event => {
      if (
        panelRef.current &&
        !panelRef.current.contains(event.target) &&
        toggleBtnRef.current &&
        !toggleBtnRef.current.contains(event.target)
      ) {
        closeMenu();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, [closeOnClickAway, open, closeMenu]);

  return (
    <div
      className={(className ? className + ' ' : '') + 'staggered-menu-wrapper' + (isFixed ? ' fixed-wrapper' : '')}
      style={accentColor ? { ['--sm-accent']: accentColor } : undefined}
      data-position={position}
      data-open={open || undefined}
    >
      <div ref={preLayersRef} className="sm-prelayers" aria-hidden="true">
        {(() => {
          const raw = colors && colors.length ? colors.slice(0, 4) : ['#1e1e22', '#35353c'];
          let arr = [...raw];
          if (arr.length >= 3) {
            const mid = Math.floor(arr.length / 2);
            arr.splice(mid, 1);
          }
          return arr.map((c, i) => <div key={i} className="sm-prelayer" style={{ background: c }} />);
        })()}
      </div>
      <header className="staggered-menu-header" aria-label="Main navigation header">
        <div className="sm-logo" aria-label="Logo">
          <img
            src={logoUrl || '/src/assets/logos/reactbits-gh-white.svg'}
            alt="Logo"
            className="sm-logo-img"
            draggable={false}
            width={110}
            height={24}
          />
        </div>
        <button
          ref={toggleBtnRef}
          className="sm-toggle"
          aria-label={open ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu'}
          aria-expanded={open}
          aria-controls="staggered-menu-panel"
          onClick={toggleMenu}
          type="button"
        >
          <span ref={textWrapRef} className="sm-toggle-textWrap" aria-hidden="true">
            <span ref={textInnerRef} className="sm-toggle-textInner">
              {textLines.map((l, i) => (
                <span className="sm-toggle-line" key={i}>
                  {l}
                </span>
              ))}
            </span>
          </span>
          <span ref={iconRef} className="sm-icon" aria-hidden="true">
            <span ref={plusHRef} className="sm-icon-line" />
            <span ref={plusVRef} className="sm-icon-line sm-icon-line-v" />
          </span>
        </button>
      </header>

      <aside id="staggered-menu-panel" ref={panelRef} className="staggered-menu-panel" aria-hidden={!open}>
        <div className="sm-panel-inner">
          <ul className="sm-panel-list" role="list" data-numbering={displayItemNumbering || undefined}>
            {items && items.length ? (
              items.map((it, idx) => (
                <li className="sm-panel-itemWrap" key={it.label + idx}>
                  <a className="sm-panel-item" href={it.link} aria-label={it.ariaLabel} data-index={idx + 1}>
                    <span className="sm-panel-itemLabel">{it.label}</span>
                  </a>
                </li>
              ))
            ) : (
              <li className="sm-panel-itemWrap" aria-hidden="true">
                <span className="sm-panel-item">
                  <span className="sm-panel-itemLabel">No items</span>
                </span>
              </li>
            )}
          </ul>
          {displaySocials && socialItems && socialItems.length > 0 && (
            <div className="sm-socials" aria-label="Social links">
              <h3 className="sm-socials-title">Socials</h3>
              <ul className="sm-socials-list" role="list">
                {socialItems.map((s, i) => (
                  <li key={s.label + i} className="sm-socials-item">
                    <a href={s.link} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="sm-socials-link">
                      {s.label}
                    </a>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
};

export default StaggeredMenu;
